Algorithm for full log update:
```
def log_update(p, tau, lr, weight_mode):
    c = conv(p)
    m = c.max()
    weights = exp((c - m)/tau)
    q = weights / weights.sum()
    h = correlate(q,p)  # length N
    inner = dot(p,h)
    if inner==0: return
    g = h/inner - 1
    # clip g to avoid huge exponent
    g = np.clip(g, -5.0, 5.0)
    p = p * np.exp(-lr * g)
    p = np.clip(p, 0, None)
    s=p.sum(); if s<=0: reset; p/=s
    return p, c.max(), inner
```
Does this decrease F? Not guaranteed; can use acceptance/test. Evaluate Fτ before/after; if not improved, try smaller lr or random. Since nonconvex, use line search/backtracking: try lr, if F increases, reduce.
We can also keep best p for F and final exact.

Scale: g may be O(1/N). Use `scale = max(-g.min(), g.max())`. update exponent `-lr * g / scale` yields exponents in [-lr, lr]. Choose lr ~1.0 to 0.2. This is like softmax tilt. After normalization, p changes. We can do line search on internal Fτ: try step lr, if Fτ new >= old, reduce lr.
Implement:
```
def _one_log_update(p, tau, lr, max_exp=5):
   c=conv(p); m=c.max()
   q=softmax((c-m)/tau); q/=q.sum()
   h=corr(q,p)
   inner=dot(p,h)
   if inner<=0: fail
   g=h-inner
   scale=max(abs(g).max(),1e-18)
   u=np.exp(-lr * g/scale)
   u=np.clip(u, exp(-max_exp), exp(max_exp))
   p2=p*u; s=sum; if s<=0 fail; p2/=s
   c2=conv(p2); F2=m2+tau log...
   if F2 < F: accept
   else reduce lr by 0.5 up to 5 times; if still fail, maybe random perturbation.
```
This computes c twice if line search. Could compute once and approximate; but okay if iterations limited. To save time, we can accept based on original max m2 < m + tolerance? Use simple.
Better: Compute c once for old; after update compute c2; if m2 + bias < m_old + bias accept. Since objective max; we want nonincrease in max. For nonconvex, increasing τ smooth may help but accept max. Use `score_internal=2N*m`. Accept if m2 < m - 1e-12 or occasionally random. If not, shrink.

However, local minima may require temporary increase. Use simulated annealing: accept if m2 < m + temp or random. But final maximize improvement. We can implement random restarts and hill-climbing only.

Basis construction:
```
def _make_basis(N, n_poly, n_trig, seed=0):
    t = (np.arange(N) + 0.5)/N  # cell centers 0..1? Or i/(N-1)? Use midpoints to avoid endpoints.
    rows=[]
    for r in range(1, n_poly+1):
       x=2*t-1
       row=chebyshev T_r(x) maybe np.polynomial.chebyshev.chebval? Could compute recursively to avoid import.
       rows.append(row)
    for m in range(1,n_trig+1):
       rows.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t))
       rows.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t))
    B=np.vstack(rows)
    # center each row: B -= B.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
```

```
def _lowdim_f(v,B,N,tau):
    z = B.dot(v)  # shape N
    m = np.max(z)
    expz = np.exp(z-m)
    p = expz / expz.sum()
    c = _conv_square(p, L)
    m_c = c.max()
    if c[-?]? 
    if tau <= 0: return m_c
    w = np.exp((c-m_c)/tau)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <=0: return m_c
    return m_c + tau*np.log(s)
```

```
def _lowdim_g(v,B,N,tau):

   P=np.fft.rfft(p, L)

   # numerically h may small negative; no need clip.
   inner=np.dot(p,h)
   term=B.T @ (p*h)
   bp=B.T @ p
   grad=2.0*(term - inner*bp)
   return grad
```

`_conv_square(a, L=None)`:
```
def _conv_square(a, L=None):
    n=a.size
    if L is None: L=2*n-1
    F=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)
    # numerical clip small negatives? For max no; but for q, if c has small negative, okay.
    return np.ascontiguousarray(c)
```
For p sum1, c length L.

`_softmax_values(x, tau)`: for c: `m=x.max(); y=np.exp((x-m)/tau); s=y.sum(); if s==0: return ones? else y/s`.

`_corr_qp(q, p, L)`:
```
P=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
Q=np.fft.rfft(q, L)
h=np.fft.irfft(Q*np.conj(P), L)
return h[:p.size]
```
Need ensure h orientation as above. Test with small mentally: N=2 p=[.5,.5], q length3 all? q=[0,1,0] central. P=[...], Q; h should [p1? i=0 q0 p0+q1 p1=0.5; i=1 q1p0+q2p1=0.25? Wait q=[0,1,0], h0=0* p0 +1*p1=0.5, h1=1*p0+0=0.5. Both0.5. Formula ifft(Q*conj(P)). Let's compute cyclic: q has at index1. ifft(Q*conj(P))_i =∑_m q_m p_{m-i} with zero pad: m=1 => p_{1-i}. i=0 p1=.5; i=1 p0=.5. good.

`_fit_basis(B, p)`: solve least squares `z = np.log(p+eps)`; `v = np.linalg.lstsq(B.T, z, rcond=None)[0]`? B.T shape N x D, z N. We want B.T v ≈ z. So `v = np.linalg.lstsq(B.T, z)[0]`. Center log p?
```
def _fit_basis(B,p):
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    z-=z.mean()
    A=B.T
    v=np.linalg.lstsq(A,z,rcond=None)[0]
    return v
```
D*N small.

`_make_basis(N,n_poly,n_trig)`:
```
def _make_basis(N, n_poly=12, n_trig=14):
    t=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/float(N)
    rows=[]
    x=2*t-1
    T0=np.ones(N); T1=x
    for r in range(1,n_poly+1):
       if r==1: row=T1.copy()
       elif r==2: row=2*x*T1 - T0
       else: need recurrence previously. Better iterative:
         Tf=T0, Tc=T1; for r=1..n_poly: if r==1 row=Tc; else Tn=2*x*Tc-Tf; row=Tc; Tf,Tc=Tc,Tn
    for m in 1..n_trig: row cos, sin.
    B=np.vstack(rows) if rows else np.zeros((1,N));
    B-=B.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std=B.std(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std=np.where(std<1e-12,1.0,std)
    B/=std
    return B
```
Chebyshev values grow near edges? T_r(-1)=±1, amplitude1. Good. n_poly 12, n_trig14 =>40 rows.

```
def _optimize_lowdim(v0, B, N, tau, bmax, maxiter=800, time_s=None):
    if minimize is None: return v0,_lowdim_f(...)
    D=B.shape[0]
    bounds=[(-bmax,bmax)]*D
    try:
       res=minimize(_lowdim_f, v0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_lowdim_g, args=(B,N,tau), bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':3*maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':50})
       return res.x, res.fun
    except: return ...
```

Full log gradient function:
```
def _full_log_improve(p, N, budget_s, seed=1, tau0=2e-5, tau1=2e-7, lr0=1.0, lr1=0.1, restarts=10):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = _clean_p(p,N)
    L=2*N-1
    best=p.copy()
    best_m = _internal_max(p,N)
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    accepted_run=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < budget_s:
       progress = (time.monotonic()-start)/max(budget_s,1e-9)
       tau = tau0*(tau1/tau0)**min(1.0,progress)
       lr = lr0*(lr1/lr0)**min(1.0,progress)
       c=_conv_square(p,L); m=c.max()
       q=_softmax(c,tau)
       h=_corr_qp(q,p,L)
       inner=np.dot(p,h)
       g=h-inner
       scale=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale<1e-18:
           # random restart
           p=_random_p(N, rng, mode=restart...)
           continue
       ok=False
       step=lr
       for _ in range(5):
           expo=-step*g/scale
           expo=np.clip(expo,-4,4)
           newp=p*np.exp(expo)
           newp=np.maximum(newp,0)
           s=newp.sum()
           if s<=0 or not finite: break
           newp/=s
           newc=_conv_square(newp,L)
           newm=newc.max()
           if newm < m - 1e-14:
               p=newp; m=newm; ok=True; break
           step*=0.5
       if m < best_m:
           best=p.copy(); best_m=m
       if not ok:
           # occasionally accept random perturbation or restart
           if rng.random() < 0.01:
               p *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,N))
               s=p.sum(); p/=s
           # if scale huge maybe restart
       it+=1
    return best, 2*N*best_m
```
But `m` inside if not ok may not update. Use old c; if no accept, maybe update p with small random and continue.
We should compute exact internal score as `2*N*best_m`, since p sum1. Use `evaluate_sequence` occasionally? Not needed.

Then softmax. To avoid constructing B each time, helper `_random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0, num_modes=8)`.

Algorithm:
```
def _random_block_refine(p,N,budget_s,seed, block_min, block_max, sigma=0.2):
   rng=default_rng
   best=p; best_m=...
   while time<deadline:
      cand=best.copy()
      width=rng.integers(N//100, N//10)
      start=rng.integers(0,N-width)
      cand[start:start+width] *= exp(rng.normal(0, sigma))
      # optionally random global phase? no
      cand = np.maximum(cand,0); normalize
      m=max(conv(cand))
      if m < best_m*(1-1e-10): best=cand; best_m=m
      elif rng.random() < 0.02: best=cand  # occasional diversify? maybe not
   return best
```

```
def _softmax_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   m=np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
   return e/s
```

`_random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0)`:
```
def _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0, max_mode=8):
   t=np.arange(N)/N
   z=np.zeros(N)
   for m in range(1,max_mode+1):
      amp=np.exp(rng.normal(-1.0,0.3)) # small
      phase1=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      phase2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      z += strength*amp*(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t+phase1)+np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t+phase2))
   z += strength*rng.normal(0,0.3) # optional
   return _softmax_z(z)
```
But adding full normal creates high freq peaked; maybe not. Keep only Fourier.

Pseudo:
```
def _full_log_refine(p0,N,budget_s,seed):
   p=_clean_p(p0,N)
   best=p.copy(); best_m=...
   start=time.monotonic(); elapsed=0; it=0; stale=0
   while time.monotonic()-start < budget_s:
      progress=...
      tau=...
      lr=...
      c=_conv_square(p,L); m=c.max()
      q=_softmax_c(c,tau)
      h=_corr_qp(q,p,L)
      inner=float(np.dot(p,h))
      g=h-inner
      scale=np.nanmax(np.abs(g))
      if not finite or scale<1e-20: restart
      accepted=False
      step=lr
      for __ in range(6):
         expo=np.clip(-step*g/scale, -3.0, 3.0)
         newp=p*np.exp(expo)
         newp=np.maximum(newp,0); s=newp.sum()
         if s>0:
            newp/=s
            newm=_conv_square(newp,L).max()
            if newm < m*(1-1e-12) - 1e-16:
                p=newp; m=newm; accepted=True; break
            step*=0.5
      if accepted:
         if m < best_m:
            best_m=m; best=p.copy()
         stale=0
      else:
         stale+=1
         if stale>50:
            # random nudge or restart
            if rng.random()<0.5:
                p = _softmax_z(np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0,0.2,N))
            else:
                p = _random_smooth_p(N,rng,strength=0.5)
            stale=0
      it+=1
   return best, 2*N*best_m
```

Detailed helper implementations:

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

def _soft_q(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
       q=np.zeros(c.size); q[c.size//2]=1; return q
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-18)
    y = np.exp((c - m) / tau)
    s = y.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       q = np.zeros(c.size)
       idx = np.where(np.abs(c-m) < 1e-12*max(1,m))[0]
       if idx.size == 0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
       q[idx] = 1.0/idx.size
       return q
    return y / s
```
But if tau very small, y underflows except near max; okay.

```
return 2.0*N*float(np.max(c))
```

```
def _lowdim_f(v,B,N,tau,L):
    z = B.dot(v)
    pm = np.max(z)
    expz=np.exp(z-pm)
    s=expz.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100
    p=expz/s
    c=_conv_square(p,L)
    mc=np.max(c)
    if not finite: return 1e100
    if tau <= 1e-15: return mc
    y=np.exp((c-mc)/tau)
    sy=y.sum()
    if sy<=0: return mc
    return mc + tau*np.log(sy)
```
Could `c` have tiny negative; max positive.

`_lowdim_g`: compute p; if invalid return zeros.
```
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    q=_soft_q(c,tau)
    Q=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(Q*np.conj(P), L)
    h=h[:N]
    h=np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    inner=float(np.dot(p,h))
    # if inner not finite, 1? 
    ph=p*h
    term=B.T.dot(ph)
    bp=B.T.dot(p)
    inner = inner if np.isfinite(inner) and inner>0 else 1.0
    grad=2.0*(term - inner*bp)
    if not all finite: return zeros(D)
    return grad
```

```
def _block_refine(p,N,deadline,seed, sym_prob=0.2):
   p=_clean_p(p,N); L=2N-1; best=p; best_m=2N*max(...)
   rng=...

   while time<deadline:
      candidate = best.copy()
      minw=max(1,N//500); maxw=max(minw+1, N//20)
      w=int(rng.integers(minw,maxw))
      s0=int(rng.integers(0,N-w+1)) if N>w else 0
      factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, size=w))
      candidate[s0:s0+w] *= factor
      # occasionally add small random global: candidate *= exp(rng.normal(0,0.05)) but sum change irrelevant
      if rng.random()<0.2: candidate = 0.5*(candidate+candidate[::-1])
      candidate=np.maximum(candidate,0); s=candidate.sum()
      if s>0: candidate/=s
      m=_conv_square(candidate,L).max()
      if m < best_m*(1-1e-10):
         best=candidate; best_m=m
      else:
         # if no improvement for many, reduce sigma? Maybe track count.
```

Let's implement `_make_basis` carefully:
```
def _make_basis(N, n_poly=12, n_trig=14):
    if N <= 0: return None
    n_poly = max(0, int(n_poly))
    n_trig = max(0, int(n_trig))
    t = (np.arange(N, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(N)
    x = 2.0*t - 1.0
    rows=[]
    if n_poly > 0:
        Tprev = np.ones(N)
        Tcur = x.copy()
        for r in range(1, n_poly+1):
            rows.append(Tcur.copy())
            if r < n_poly:
                Tnext = 2.0*x*Tcur - Tprev
                Tprev, Tcur = Tcur, Tnext
    for m in range(1, n_trig+1):
        angle=2*np.pi*m*t
        rows.append(np.cos(angle))
        rows.append(np.sin(angle))
    if not rows:
        B=np.zeros((1,N), float)
    else:
        B=np.vstack(rows).astype(float, copy=False)
    B = B - B.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std = B.std(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std = np.where(std > 1e-12, std, 1.0)
    B = B / std
    # If all zero? keep
    return B
```
For r=1, row T1; after append, if r<n_poly compute Tnext for next r. Works.

`_random_smooth_p`:
```
def _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0, max_mode=10, use_poly=False):
    t=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    z=np.zeros(N)
    for m in range(1, max_mode+1):
        amp=np.exp(rng.normal(-1.0,0.4)) * strength
        ph1=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); ph2=...
        z += amp*(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + ph1) + np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t + ph2))
    # add low poly linear
    z += strength*rng.normal(0,0.5)*(2*t-1)
    return _softmax_z(z)
```
If N small, max_mode use min(10,N//2).

```
def _random_sparse_p(N,rng,density=0.5):
    p=np.full(N, 1e-12, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(3): # clusters
       k=int(rng.integers(max(1,N//20), N))
       idx=set? Instead mask density* rng.random < density
    mask = rng.random(N) < density
    p[mask] = 1.0 + 5.0*rng.random(int(mask.sum()))
    # maybe geometric edge mask
    p /= sum
    return p
```

```
def _finalize(pool,N):
   best_seq=None; best_exact=np.inf
   for p in pool:
      p=_clean_p(p,N); lst=[float(x) for x in p]
      try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
      except: sc=np.inf
      if sc<best_exact: best_exact=sc; best_seq=lst
   if best_seq is None: best_seq=[float(x) for x in _clean_p(best_p,N)]
   return best_seq
```

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-function sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a) ** 2)

for the given construction length (typically len(height_sequence_1)).

Method:
1. Work with normalized sequences sum(a) = 1, so the score is 2*n*max(a*a).
2. Use a smooth low-dimensional softmax parameterization and minimize a
   log-sum-exp surrogate of max(a*a) with L-BFGS-B.
3. Polish in full log-coordinate space using multiplicative subgradient
   updates on the active top coefficients.
4. Finish with randomized block perturbations for fine-tuning.
The best exact candidate is selected with the provided evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


def _conv_square(a, L):
    """Full square convolution a*a, length L=2*n-1, via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
    return c


def _score_internal(p, N):
    """Internal score for a length-N positive vector p."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != N or p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    c = _conv_square(p, 2 * N - 1)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * N * m)


def _clean_p(p, N):
    """Clean and normalize a vector to the simplex."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != N or p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def _softmax_z(z):
    """Stable softmax."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    m = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones_like(z) / float(z.size)
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _soft_q(c, tau):
    """Openly normalized softmax weights over convolution coefficients."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return c
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        q = np.zeros(n)
        q[0] = 1.0
        return q
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)
    y = np.exp((c - m) / tau)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.zeros(n)
        idx = np.where(c >= m - 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(m)))[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
        q[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return q
    return y / s


def _corr_qp(q, p, L):
    """
    h[i] = sum_k q[k] * p[k-i], for i=0..N-1.
    This is the required correlation for the derivative of a*a.
    """
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Q * np.conj(P), L)
    return h[:p.size]


def _make_basis(N, n_poly=12, n_trig=14):
    """
    A centered/standardized basis of Chebyshev polynomials and trigonometric
    modes for the vector log-heights.
    """
    N = max(1, int(N))
    n_poly = max(0, int(n_poly))
    n_trig = max(0, int(n_trig))
    t = (np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(N)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    rows = []

    if n_poly > 0:
        Tprev = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
        Tcur = x.copy()
        for r in range(1, n_poly + 1):
            rows.append(Tcur.copy())
            if r < n_poly:
                Tnext = 2.0 * x * Tcur - Tprev
                Tprev, Tcur = Tcur, Tnext

    for m in range(1, n_trig + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t
        rows.append(np.cos(ang))
        rows.append(np.sin(ang))

    if not rows:
        B = np.zeros((1, N), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        B = np.vstack(rows).astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    B = B - B.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std = B.std(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std = np.where(std > 1e-12, std, 1.0)
    B = B / std
    return B


def _fit_basis(B, p):
    """Least-squares fit B.T @ v ~ log(p), removing the constant part."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    D = B.shape[0]
    if D == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    A = B.T
    try:
        v = np.linalg.lstsq(A, z, rcond=None)[0]
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        return v
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)


def _lowdim_f(v, B, N, tau, L):
    """Log-sum-exp surrogate objective for a low-dimensional softmax model."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    z = B.dot(v)
    p = _softmax_z(z)
    c = _conv_square(p, L)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return 1e100
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)
    y = np.exp((c - mc) / tau)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return mc
    return mc + tau * np.log(s)


def _lowdim_g(v, B, N, tau, L):
    """Analytic gradient of _lowdim_f with respect to v."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    D = B.shape[0]
    z = B.dot(v)
    p = _softmax_z(z)
    c = _conv_square(p, L)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)

    q = _soft_q(c, tau)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Q * np.conj(P), L)[:N]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

    inner = float(np.dot(p, h))
    if not np.isfinite(inner) or inner <= 0.0:
        inner = 1.0

    ph = p * h
    term = B.T.dot(ph)
    bp = B.T.dot(p)
    grad = 2.0 * (term - inner * bp)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad


def _optimize_lowdim(v0, B, N, tau, L, bmax, maxiter):
    """One L-BFGS-B run on the low-dimensional surrogate."""
    v0 = np.asarray(v0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if minimize is None:
        return v0, _lowdim_f(v0, B, N, tau, L)

    D = B.shape[0]
    v0 = np.nan_to_num(v0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    bmax = max(0.1, float(bmax))
    bounds = [(-bmax, bmax)] * D
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lowdim_f,
            v0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=_lowdim_g,
            args=(B, N, tau, L),
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(3 * maxiter + 10),
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 40,
            },
        )
        v1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        v1 = np.nan_to_num(v1, nan=0.0, posinf=bmax, neginf=-bmax)
        v1 = np.clip(v1, -bmax, bmax)
        if _lowdim_f(v1, B, N, tau, L) < _lowdim_f(v0, B, N, tau, L):
            return v1, float(res.fun)
        return v0, _lowdim_f(v0, B, N, tau, L)
    except Exception:
        return v0, _lowdim_f(v0, B, N, tau, L)


def _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0, max_mode=10):
    """Random smooth positive sequence in softmax log coordinates."""
    N = max(1, int(N))
    max_mode = max(1, min(int(max_mode), max(1, N // 2)))
    t = (np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(N)
    z = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, max_mode + 1):
        amp = float(np.exp(rng.normal(-1.0, 0.35)))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t
        z += strength * amp * (np.cos(ang + ph1) + np.sin(ang + ph2))
    z += strength * float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    return _softmax_z(z)


def _soft_perturb_p(p, rng, alpha=0.25):
    """Perturb log(p) with small full-dimensional noise."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    N = p.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z += float(alpha) * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, N)
    return _softmax_z(z)


def _full_log_refine(p0, N, budget_s, seed=1, tau_frac0=0.12,
                     tau_frac1=0.003, lr0=0.8, lr1=0.06):
    """
    Full length iterative multiplicative subgradient refinement in log-space.
    Returns (best_p, best_internal_score).
    """
    p = _clean_p(p0, N)
    N = int(N)
    L = 2 * N - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    c = _conv_square(p, L)
    best_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_p = p.copy()
    cur_m = best_m

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    it = 0
    stale = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        total = max(1e-12, deadline - start)
        prog = min(1.0, elapsed / total)

        tau = max(1e-300, (tau_frac0 * (1.0 - prog) + tau_frac1 * prog) / float(N))
        lr = lr0 * (lr1 / lr0) ** prog

        c = _conv_square(p, L)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        q = _soft_q(c, tau)
        h = _corr_qp(q, p, L)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

        inner = float(np.dot(p, h))
        if not np.isfinite(inner) or inner <= 1e-300:
            p = _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0)
            stale = 0
            continue

        g = h / inner - 1.0
        g = np.clip(g, -3.0, 3.0)

        accepted = False
        step = lr
        for _ in range(7):
            expo = np.clip(-step * g, -6.0, 6.0)
            cand = p * np.exp(expo)
            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
            s = float(np.sum(cand))
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                step *= 0.5
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            cm = _conv_square(cand, L)
            nm = float(np.max(cm))
            if not np.isfinite(nm):
                step *= 0.5
                continue
            # Accept clear improvements, and occasionally small uphill moves.
            if nm < m - 1e-14:
                p = cand
                m = nm
                cur_m = nm
                accepted = True
                break
            if rng.random() < 0.03 and nm < m + 1e-11:
                p = cand
                m = nm
                cur_m = nm
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 40:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    p = _soft_perturb_p(best_p, rng, alpha=0.35)
                else:
                    p = _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.0)
                p = _clean_p(p, N)
                cm = _conv_square(p, L)
                m = float(np.max(cm))
                if np.isfinite(m):
                    cur_m = m
                stale = 0

        if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_m:
            best_m = m
            best_p = p.copy()

        it += 1

    return best_p, float(2.0 * N * best_m)


def _block_refine(p0, N, deadline, seed=1):
    """Random block multiplicative local search with occasional annealed jumps."""
    p = _clean_p(p0, N)
    N = int(N)
    L = 2 * N - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    c = _conv_square(p, L)
    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_m = cur_m
    best_p = p.copy()

    no_improve = 0
    min_w = max(2, N // 800)
    max_w = max(min_w + 2, N // 12)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed_progress = 0.0  # approximate full schedule by no_improve anneal
        if no_improve > 0:
            sigma = max(0.01, 0.55 * np.exp(-0.003 * float(no_improve)))
        else:
            sigma = 0.55

        cand = best_p.copy()
        w = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w + 1))
        if N <= w:
            w = max(2, N // 4)
        s0 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, N - w + 1)))
        e1 = min(N, s0 + w)
        cand[s0:e1] *= np.exp(sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, e1 - s0))

        if rng.random() < 0.18:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            continue
        cand = cand / s
        cm = _conv_square(cand, L)
        nm = float(np.max(cm))
        if not np.isfinite(nm):
            continue

        # Use cand as the walker if it is not clearly worse than best.
        if nm < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            best_p = cand
            best_m = nm
            cur_m = nm
            no_improve = 0
        elif nm < best_m + 1e-9 * max(1.0, best_m) and rng.random() < 0.10:
            cur_m = nm
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                # Update walker but not best, for diversity.
                p = cand
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 120:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    p = _soft_perturb_p(best_p, rng, alpha=0.35)
                else:
                    p = _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.2)
                p = _clean_p(p, N)
                cm = _conv_square(p, L)
                pm = float(np.max(cm))
                if np.isfinite(pm):
                    cur_m = pm
                no_improve = 0

        # Keep p merely for potential future perturbations; best_p drives search.
        if np.isfinite(cur_m) and cur_m < best_m:
            best_p = _clean_p(p, N)
            best_m = cur_m

    return best_p, float(2.0 * N * best_m)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800, seed=17, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a low-score nonnegative sequence. Always returns a valid list.
    """
    start0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = start0 + budget - 6.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Determine working length. Prefer existing construction length when sane.
    N = 6121
    parent_arr = None
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
                arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
                if arr.sum() > 0.0:
                    if 1 <= arr.size <= 50000:
                        N = int(arr.size)
                        parent_arr = arr
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    L = 2 * N - 1

    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size == N:
        best_p = _clean_p(parent_arr, N)
    else:
        best_p = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)

    best_score = _score_internal(best_p, N)
    print("initial N=%d score=%.9f" % (N, best_score), flush=True)

    pool = [best_p.copy()]

    def _note(p, sc):
        nonlocal best_p, best_score
        p = _clean_p(p, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_p = p
            best_score = float(sc)
            if len(pool) < 12:
                pool.append(p.copy())
            print("improved score=%.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # Low-dimensional smooth optimization, if N is large enough.
    if N >= 50 and minimize is not None:
        n_poly = min(12, max(5, N // 500))
        n_trig = min(18, max(5, N // 400))
        B = _make_basis(N, n_poly=n_poly, n_trig=n_trig)
        D = B.shape[0]

        tau_fracs = [0.25, 0.10, 0.04, 0.016, 0.006, 0.002]
        bmaxs = [1.0, 1.6, 2.2, 3.0, 3.8, 4.6]
        maxiter = 140

        starts = []
        starts.append(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))
        for a in [0.7, 1.2, 2.0, 3.0, -0.7, -1.2, -2.0, -3.0]:
            v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
            if D > 0:
                v[0] = float(a)
            starts.append(v)
        for a in [1.0, -1.0]:
            v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
            if D > 1:
                v[1] = float(a)
            starts.append(v)
        try:
            starts.append(np.clip(_fit_basis(B, best_p), -2.0, 2.0))
        except Exception:
            pass
        for _ in range(28):
            starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, D))

        lowdim_deadline = min(hard_deadline, start0 + min(360.0, 0.25 * budget))
        idx = 0
        while idx < len(starts) and time.monotonic() < lowdim_deadline:
            v0 = starts[idx]
            idx += 1
            v = v0.copy()

            for j, frac in enumerate(tau_fracs):
                if time.monotonic() >= lowdim_deadline:
                    break
                tau = max(1e-12, frac / float(N))
                bmax = bmaxs[min(j, len(bmaxs) - 1)]
                v, _ = _optimize_lowdim(v, B, N, tau, L, bmax, maxiter)

            p = _softmax_z(B.dot(v))
            sc = _score_internal(p, N)
            _note(p, sc)

            # If this start improved, add a basin around its fitted vector.
            if best_score < 1.8:
                try:
                    vfit = np.clip(_fit_basis(B, best_p), -3.5, 3.5)
                    starts.append(vfit + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, D))
                except Exception:
                    pass

        print("after lowdim score=%.9f time=%.1fs" %
              (best_score, time.monotonic() - start0), flush=True)

    # Full-space log-coordinate refinement, with a few restarts.
    full_end = min(hard_deadline, start0 + max(120.0, 0.72 * budget - 120.0))
    rcount = 0
    while time.monotonic() < full_end - 25:
        rem = full_end - time.monotonic() - 2.0
        this_budget = min(140.0, max(30.0, rem))
        if rcount == 0:
            p0 = best_p.copy()
        elif rcount % 3 == 1:
            p0 = _soft_perturb_p(best_p, rng, alpha=0.35)
        elif rcount % 3 == 2:
            p0 = _random_smooth_p(N, rng, strength=1.1)
        else:
            p0 = best_p.copy()

        p_new, sc_new = _full_log_refine(
            p0, N, this_budget,
            seed=900 + 17 * rcount,
            tau_frac0=0.12,
            tau_frac1=0.003,
            lr0=0.85,
            lr1=0.055,
        )
        _note(p_new, sc_new)
        rcount += 1

    print("after full log score=%.9f time=%.1fs" %
          (best_score, time.monotonic() - start0), flush=True)

    # Final randomized block refinement on the best sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20:
        p_new, sc_new = _block_refine(best_p, N, hard_deadline - 15.0,
                                      seed=4242)
        _note(p_new, sc_new)

    print("before final exact score=%.9f time=%.1fs" %
          (best_score, time.monotonic() - start0), flush=True)

    # Exact final selection among a few candidate sequences.
    candidates = list(pool)
    if best_p not in candidates:
        candidates.append(best_p)

    final_best = None
    final_exact = np.inf
    # Limit exact evaluations based on length to avoid excessive direct convolution.
    max_exact = 10 if N <= 12000 else 2
    for p in candidates[:max_exact]:
        p = _clean_p(p, N)
        seq = [float(x) for x in p]
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_exact:
            final_exact = sc
            final_best = seq

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = [float(x) for x in _clean_p(best_p, N)]

    return final_best
```
